Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the meteorology structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 628. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 579 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the meteorology decomposition. By minimality, the element a^628 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 579-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
